Abstract: Immersed-boundary lattice Boltzmann (IB-LBM) simulations of cylinder and particle flows are usually compared by ranking boundary-enforcement schemes, kernels, and collision models. Such comparisons can obscure the mechanism that controls each response. Here, canonical fixed-cylinder, oscillating-cylinder, and sedimenting-particle cases are used as controlled probes of Eulerian-Lagrangian interface momentum transfer. For prescribed bodies, differences among direct forcing (DF), multi-direct forcing (MDF), and distribution-function correction (DFC) are most clearly discriminated by local no-slip fidelity rather than by a universal drag ranking. For DFC, the kernel-dependent drag-coefficient ranking reversal between the hat and Peskin 4-point kernels is associated with the spatial redistribution of the marker-resolved correction and the resulting near-boundary slip and pressure deviation, rather than the total correction magnitude alone. For sedimenting particles, single-particle settling provides a moving-body baseline, whereas two-particle differential-density wake-interaction sedimentation shows the wake-exposed light particle to be comparatively more sensitive to the explicit internal-mass correction in the force evaluation, a configuration-dependent, finite-window moving-boundary closure response. Targeted two-relaxation-time (TRT) and central-moment multiple-relaxation-time (CM-MRT) collision controls remain secondary to the boundary and closure mechanisms within the tested regimes (prescribed-body comparisons at Re <= 200, two-dimensional, and the reported moving-particle cases). The resulting picture replaces a universal scheme ranking with a mechanism-resolved interpretation of IB-LBM interface momentum transfer.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往对通气空化的数值研究通常忽略表面张力的影响，且缺乏对注入位置如何影响空腔生成与演化的系统研究。理解注入位置对空腔稳定性及阻力特性的作用机理，对于通气减阻技术的工程应用具有重要价值。

Method: 采用DNS方法对球体绕流通气空化进行数值模拟（Re=10,000），将流动建模为水与空气的均匀混合物，使用VOF方法追踪界面，并显式计入表面张力效应。空气通过球体表面的环形条带注入，注入位置依据主流体动力学选取三处：前部(FR025, 18°≤θ≤63°)、中部(MD025, MD050, 75°≤θ≤104°)和背部(BK025, 120°≤θ≤180°)，流量系数C_q为0.2和0.4。

Result: 注入位置强烈决定空腔的生成与稳定性。前部注入因剧烈的喷涌(puffing)产生不稳定的气泡状空腔，使注入空气破碎，阻力较单相流增加约56%。中部与背部注入则产生具有不同前缘动力学的稳定空腔。中部注入以喷涌为主导，虽延迟流动分离，但空腔从前缘分离后脱附；背部注入的空腔因空腔形成诱导的逆压梯度而向上游脱附，并呈现开尔文-亥姆霍兹不稳定和凹坑形成特征。稳定空腔具有强烈的空气夹带并通过再入射流闭合。中部注入在C_q=0.2和0.4时分别实现35%和25%的减阻，背部注入最大减阻达46%。

Conclusion: 注入位置是控制通气空化行为与减阻效果的关键参数。背部注入产生最稳定空腔并获得最大减阻(46%)，中部注入也能实现显著减阻，而前部注入反而导致阻力增加。研究揭示了不同注入位置下空腔形成-脱附-闭合的机理差异，为通气减阻技术的参数优化提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We perform DNS of ventilated cavitation over a sphere at subcritical (Re=10,000). The flow is modeled as a homogeneous mixture of water and air, with the interface tracked using the volume-of-fluid method. Unlike previous numerical investigations, surface tension is explicitly included. Air is injected through a circular strip on the sphere at three locations selected based on the bulk flow dynamics: front (FR025, (18^\circ\leqθ\leq63^\circ)), mid (MD025, MD050, (75^\circ\leqθ\leq104^\circ)), and back (BK025, (120^\circ\leqθ\leq180^\circ)), with (C_q=0.2) and (0.4). The injection location strongly governs cavity inception and stability and alters flow separation in the front- and mid-injection cases. Front injection produces an unsteady bubbly cavity due to vigorous puffing, which fragments the injected air and increases drag by (\sim56%) relative to the single-phase case. In contrast, mid- and back-injection produce stable cavities with distinct leading-edge dynamics. In the mid-injection cases, puffing dominates and, despite delayed flow separation, the cavity detaches from the leading edge of the injection patch. For back injection, the cavity detaches upstream of the injection patch owing to the adverse pressure gradient induced by cavity formation. Kelvin--Helmholtz instabilities and divot formation characterize the back-injection cavity. Stable cavities exhibit strong air entrainment and close through a re-entrant jet. The mid-injection cases achieve (35%) and (25%) drag reduction at (C_q=0.2) and (0.4), respectively, while back injection yields a maximum drag reduction of (46%) relative to the single-phase case.

*Ofek Frank-Shapir,Igal Gluzman*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文在结构化小增益定理框架下提出一种新的固定结构化不确定性表示方法，通过输入/输出通道的线性变换，使非线性对流项对应的反馈不确定性具有重复对角结构，用于不可压缩剪切流（Couette流和平面Poiseuille流）的有限幅值扰动稳定性分析。该方法得到的稳定性阈值比先前的重复/非重复块近似更不保守、更准确，且伪能量产生项最小，更忠实于Navier-Stokes系统非线性反馈的本质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在不可压缩剪切流的非线性稳定性分析中，基于结构化小增益定理的方法需要将Navier-Stokes方程中的非线性对流项表示为结构化反馈不确定性。先前提出的重复和非重复块结构近似会产生伪能量产生项，违反无散约束，导致稳定性阈值保守或不准确。作者旨在构造一种新的不确定性表示方式，既能保留非线性对流项的逐分量路径信息，又能使结构化奇异值的计算保持可处理性，从而提高稳定性分析的精确度和保真度。

Method: 通过输入和输出通道的线性变换，将反馈回路重新构造，使得所得的结构化不确定性具有重复对角结构（repeated-diagonal structure）。该线性变换后的结构在保持非线性对流项分量间通路信息的同时，使结构化奇异值计算可处理。将方法应用于Couette流和平面Poiseuille流两种典型基流，并与先前提出的重复块和非重复块近似方法进行系统对比。

Result: 新方法得到的有限幅值扰动稳定性阈值比先前方法更保守性更低（less conservative）且更精确，与先前数值和实验研究吻合最好。此外，分析表明先前研究提出的重复和非重复块结构在结构化输入输出框架中使用常数结构化不确定性时会产生人为的能量产生项，违反无散假设；而本方法所产生的这一伪能量产生项最小，因而对非线性反馈与线性化动力学之间耦合的描述更为忠实。

Conclusion: 本文提出的重复对角结构化不确定性表示方法在不可压缩剪切流的鲁棒稳定性分析中优于先前提出的块结构近似方案，不仅降低了保守性、提高了稳定性阈值的准确性，而且最大程度减少了人为能量伪项，为基于结构化小增益定理的非线性稳定性分析提供了一种更为可靠的建模框架。

Abstract: This work employs a new form of fixed structured uncertainty within the structured small-gain theorem approach proposed by Frank-Shapir & Gluzman (J. Fluid Mech., vol. 1030, 2026, pp A8) for the stability analysis of incompressible shear flows subject to finite-magnitude disturbances. Within this framework, the nonlinear advection term in the Navier-Stokes equations is replaced by a structured feedback uncertainty interconnection with the linearized dynamics to account for the impact of nonlinear feedback. Herein, a new uncertainty representation is derived via linear transformations of the input and output channels, transforming the feedback loop such that the resulting structured uncertainty has a repeated-diagonal structure. This structure aims to preserve the component-wise pathways of the nonlinear advection term while keeping the structured singular value computation tractable. We apply the method to two canonical base flows: Couette and plane Poiseuille flows. The resulting thresholds on disturbance magnitude to preserve stability are less conservative and more accurate. We compare the novel methodology presented here with previously proposed repeated and non-repeated block approximations of the uncertainty structure, where our stability threshold provided the closest agreement with previous numerical and experimental studies. We show that repeated and non-repeated block structures that were proposed in past studies result in an artificial energy-production term arising from using constant structured uncertainty in the structured input-output formulation, violating the divergencefree assumption. This energy-production term is smallest when using the methodology presented in this work, providing a more faithful representation of the impact of nonlinear feedback interconnection with the linearized dynamics of the Navier-Stokes system.

</details>

Conclusion: 凸起强制起爆方法能够提供可靠的斜爆震起爆和稳定的斜爆震流场，相比于楔形诱导起爆具有明显的工程应用优势。

Abstract: The initiation and propagation mechanisms of kerosene-fueled oblique detonation waves are key issues in the development of oblique detonation wave engines. In this study, numerical simulations of kerosene-fueled oblique detonation at a flight Mach number of 10 were conducted using the two-dimensional conservative Euler equations and a second-order two-step global chemical reaction model. The objective was to compare wedge-induced initiation with bump-forced initiation. The results show that, at different flight altitudes, the oblique detonation flow field follows the \r{ho}L binary scaling law. Wedge-induced initiation requires highly precise matching of parameters such as the inlet-exit conditions, wedge angle and length, and equivalence ratio. In contrast, bump-forced initiation exploits the high total temperature and total pressure at the stagnation point and therefore does not require precise matching of these parameters. Therefore, the bump-forced initiation method provides reliable oblique detonation initiation and a stable oblique detonation flow field.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚变堆冷却包层中的液态金属在强磁场下往往被抑制为层流或准二维（Q2D）流，导致传热性能下降。作者希望探究涡流发生器能否在液态金属管道流中维持Q2D湍流态，并评估不同壁面导电条件和传热模式下该方案的可行性及换热性能。

Method: 采用三维直接数值模拟（DNS），针对导电与绝缘壁的液态金属管道流，引入涡流发生器以激励扰动。设置Shercliff壁面恒定热流边界以模拟热负荷，在固定Reynolds、Hartmann和Prandtl数下开展参数化研究，对管道水平/垂直放置情形进行分类比较。通过湍动能（TKE）和Nusselt数分析湍流输运与换热性能，并绘制流动相图。

Result: 绝缘壁条件下扰动可以被持续维持；导电壁条件下扰动可能被立即消除并转变为Walker型或Hunt型流动（伴随自身的射流脱离不稳定性）。导电管道中涡流发生器无额外增益；水平绝缘管道仅有微小效果；垂直管道中浮力引发的不稳定性产生间歇性波动，对流动结构影响显著。分析表明TKE最大的流动不一定具有最佳换热性能——侧向射流比体积混合能更快地带走热量。浮力与壁面导电率比是决定流动结构的关键因素，最终构建了涵盖不同流动状态的相图。

Conclusion: 涡流发生器维持Q2D态的可行性强烈依赖于壁面导电条件：绝缘壁下可有效维持扰动以强化换热，而导电壁下该方法失效并回归经典的MHD管道流型。浮力在垂直管道中可通过不稳定性产生额外扰动效应，但最大的湍动能并非换热最优指标，说明流场结构（如射流）对热输运的影响机制比单纯的湍流强度更重要。研究结尾提供的相图为不同工况下的流动状态选择提供了系统性参考。

Abstract: We use 3D direct numerical simulations to study the effects of vortex promoters in liquid metal duct flow to sustain Q2D states which are formed in the presence of strong magnetic fields, such as those present in cooling blankets of fusion reactors. Both for electrically insulating and conducting walls. In case of insulating walls, disturbances are found to be sustained. For conducting walls, the disturbances may be extinguished immediately and replaced by a Walker- or Hunt-type flow with its own jet-detachment instability. Furthermore, we add heat transfer by imposing constant heat flux at the Shercliff walls. We classify and compare possible configurations with respect to their turbulent transport properties. For conducting ducts, no additional effect is observed. For insulating ducts in horizontal position, we observe a small effect. For vertical ducts, the buoyancy forces have a significant impact due to buoyancy-driven instabilities, which produce intermittent fluctuations. The analysis of the turbulent kinetic energy (TKE) and the Nusselt number show that flows with the largest TKE may not have the best heat transfer performance. This is caused by side jets removing heat faster than mixing the bulk flow. The buoyancy force and wall conductance ratio are found to play a key role in determining the flow structure. Part of our work is a parametric study at fixed Reynolds, Hartmann and Prandtl numbers, which allows us eventually to compose a phase diagram showcasing the different flow regimes.

</details>


### [78] [Mapping-based exact-integral formulation of skin-friction transformations for zero-pressure-gradient compressible turbulent boundary layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02650)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期以来，预测可压缩边界层表面摩擦的标准做法是将Cf和Reθ映射到其"不可压缩"对应量上。然而本文通过广泛DNS数据库的重新评估发现，现有公式即使改善了数据坍塌性，也无法一致地恢复参考不可压缩表面摩擦行为。作者旨在建立一种基于明确定义映射的精确积分框架，以修正经典van Driest理论在有限雷诺数下的系统性局限性。

Method: (1) 将映射的"不可压缩"状态定义为物理可压缩边界层的常物性对应物，从指定的平均速度和壁面法向坐标映射推导变换因子（定义优先方法）；(2) 将van Driest理论重铸为有限雷诺数精确积分形式，经典vD I和II变换作为其首阶渐近约化被恢复；(3) 精确积分形式衍生的修正变换，通过先验缩放和后验预测进行系统评估。

Result: 经典vD II变换的历史成功被追溯为截断误差的偶然抵消；基于VIPL的修正变换在M∞=0.30至13.64、壁温比Θ=-0.55至2.85范围内表现最优，预测误差均低于11%，平均误差为3.07%。

Conclusion: 该研究将皮肤摩擦变换置于基于映射的精确积分基础之上，使其直接关联于规定的平均流映射，同时规避了限制经典van Driest理论在有限雷诺数下应用的渐近首阶截断误差，为高速边界层阻力预测提供了更严谨的理论框架。

Abstract: A long-standing route to efficient surface-drag prediction in zero-pressure-gradient compressible turbulent boundary layers is to map the skin-friction coefficient $C_f$ and momentum-thickness Reynolds number $Re_θ$ onto their `incompressible' counterparts. Reassessment against an extensive DNS database shows that existing formulations do not consistently recover the reference incompressible skin-friction behaviour, even when transformed data exhibit improved collapse. We define the mapped `incompressible' state as a constant-property counterpart of the physical compressible boundary layer and derive the transformation factors from prescribed mean-velocity and wall-normal-coordinate mappings. This definition-first approach links skin-friction scaling to the full-layer accuracy of the underlying velocity transformation and exposes inherited outer-layer errors. Van Driest's theory is recast in a finite-Re exact-integral form, with the classical vD I and II transformations recovered as leading-order asymptotic reductions. Their limitations at finite Reynolds numbers are quantified, and the historical success of vD II is traced to a fortuitous cancellation of truncation errors. The exact-integral formulation then yields modified transformations assessed through a priori scaling and standalone a posteriori prediction of $C_f$ from prescribed macroscopic and wall-thermal inputs. The VIPL-based modified transformation gives the best overall performance. Across $0.30 \leq M_\infty \leq 13.64$ and $-0.55 \leq \varTheta \leq 2.85$, its prediction errors remain below $11\%$, with a mean error of $3.07\%$. Overall, the analysis places skin-friction transformations on a mapping-based exact-integral footing, relating them directly to prescribed mean-flow mappings while avoiding the leading-order asymptotic truncations that limit classical van Driest theory at finite Reynolds numbers.

</details>


### [79] [Super-Resolution Initialization of High-Fidelity CFD Simulations for Pebble-Bed Reactors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02656)
*Guilherme Gottems,Vasil Ivakimov,Luiz Aldeia Machado,Mahmoud Yaseen,Tri Nguyen,Elia Merzari,Riccardo Balin,Dillon Shaver,Filippo Simini,Bethany Lusch,Venkatram Vishwanath,Haomin Yuan,Misun Min,Ramesh Balakrishnan,Jun Fang,Paul Fischer*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文研究了使用超分辨率图神经网络（SR-GNN）来改善球床反应器中高阶NekRS CFD模拟的初始化。通过将低阶P=2速度场重建为高阶场并作为P=7重启模拟的初始条件，在146球床（Re=1000/2500/5000）和1568球床中验证了该方法的有效性，结果表明SR-GNN初始化可减少达到统计稳态所需的流量发展阶段成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高阶CFD模拟能精细解析球床反应器中的异质性间质流，但其计算成本高昂，特别是在达到统计稳态所需的初始流动发展阶段。如何加速这一初始阶段从而降低整体计算成本是本文的核心动机。

Method: 采用SR-GNN将低阶P=2速度场重建为高阶速度场，并将这些重构场作为P=7高阶重启模拟的初始条件。模型使用配对的低阶和高阶快照进行训练，首先通过定性推理比较进行验证，然后以压降收敛为主要评价指标，在146球床的Re=1000、2500和5000三种工况下进行评估，并将训练好的Re=5000模型推广应用到1568球床上。

Result: 在Re=1000和Re=2500时，SR-GNN初始化的压降历史与从真实P=2场直接重启的结果相似；在Re=5000时，超分辨率场重启比直接P=2重启和均匀场初始化的P=7参考模拟更快地趋近统计稳态的P=7压降范围。Re=5000模型在1568球床上也展现出定性适用性。

Conclusion: SR-GNN初始化是降低高阶流动发展成本的有效策略，但需要进一步研究更广泛的雷诺数和几何外推能力。

Abstract: High-order CFD simulations provide detailed resolution of the heterogeneous interstitial flow in pebble-bed reactors, but their computational cost is high, especially during the initial flow-development period required to reach statistically stationary conditions. This work investigates the use of a Super-Resolution Graph Neural Network (SR-GNN) to improve the initialization of high-order NekRS simulations. Lower-order P = 2 velocity fields are used as inputs to reconstruct higher-order representations, which are then used as initial conditions for P = 7 restart simulations. The approach is evaluated using a 146-pebble bed at Re = 1000, Re = 2500, and Re = 5000, with pressure-drop convergence used as the main figure of merit. The SR-GNN models were trained using paired low- and high-order snapshots and were first evaluated through qualitative inference comparisons. High-order restart simulations showed that, for Re = 1000 and Re = 2500, the SR-GNN initialized cases produced pressure-drop histories similar to direct restarts from true P = 2 fields. For Re = 5000, however, the super-resolved field restart approached the statistically stationary P = 7 pressure-drop range faster than both the direct P = 2 restart and the reference P = 7 simulation initialized from a uniform velocity field. The trained Re = 5000 model was also applied to a larger 1568-pebble bed, demonstrating qualitative applicability of the workflow to a significantly larger packed-bed geometry. These results indicate that SR-GNN-based initialization is a promising strategy for reducing high-order flow-development cost, while also motivating further work on broader Reynolds-number and geometry generalization.

</details>

### [80] [Arithmetic of the sync basin for pulse-coupled oscillato](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01668)
*K. P. O'Keeffe*

TL;DR: 本文发现N个全同脉冲耦合振荡器最终落入完全同步或同步簇共存状态，其结果由N的素数分解控制。在临界充电曲线（线性）上，同步盆地获得精确的算术结构，同步概率Psync=A_{N,1}/N^N对所有N成立，其中A_{N,1}满足精确递推关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解脉冲耦合振荡器系统的最终状态（完全同步或簇同步）如何取决于系统规模N，特别是揭示临界条件下同步盆地结构的数学本质，并将数论与动力系统理论联系起来。

Method: 通过分析N个全同脉冲耦合振荡器在临界充电曲线（线性边界）上的动力学行为，引入精确递推关系来计算A_{N,1}，并利用素数分解来分析N为素数或合数时的同步概率闭式解与渐近标度。

Result: 对素数N，A_{N,1}=N^N-1，给出闭式解Psync=1-1/N^N；对合数N，渐近标度为1-Psync~C_m N^{-(m-1)}，其中m为N的最小素因子。引入了"算术盆地"这一新型盆地几何结构。

Conclusion: 该结果揭示了同步盆地可具备算术结构，为分形盆地、迷宫盆地和触手盆地的清单新增了"算术盆地"成员，建立了素数分解与脉冲耦合振荡器同步行为之间的深刻联系。

Abstract: A population of $N$ identical pulse-coupled oscillators ultimately settles into one of two outcomes: full synchrony or a state of co-existing synchronized clusters. We show that which outcome occurs is controlled by the prime factorization of $N$. At the critical charging curve --- linear, the boundary between the synchronizing and clustering regimes --- the synchronization basin acquires exact arithmetic structure. The synchronization probability is $\Psync=A_{N,1}/N^N$ for all $N$, where $A_{N,1}$ satisfies an exact recurrence relation. For prime $N$, $A_{N,1}=N^N-1$ giving the closed form $\Psync=1-1/N^N$; for composite $N$, the observed asymptotic scaling is $1-\Psync\sim C_m N^{-(m-1)}$, where $m$ is the smallest prime divisor. The result adds a new member to the atlas of exotic basin geometries: alongside fractal, riddled, and tentacled basins, we now have a basin that is arithmetic.

</details>


### [81] [Phase-delays shape multistability and basin sizes in Kuramoto networks: analytical estimates from network structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02047)

Main category: nlin.AO

TL;DR: 本文研究有限振荡器网络中网络连接性与异质相位延迟如何共同决定时空动力学状态，发现一个复合矩阵（结合连接性与相位延迟）的谱不仅决定集体状态的线性稳定性，还决定其盆地大小，从而仅凭连接性和相位延迟即可解析估计锁相态的盆地大小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相位延迟可使全局同步失稳并促进锁相模式，但连接性与相位延迟如何联合决定网络选择哪些状态仍不清楚，现有稳定性分析无法完整解释观察到的动力学现象。

Method: 提出一个结合网络连接性与相位延迟的复合矩阵，利用其谱特征来表征锁相态（包括均匀相位梯度和组合模式）的线性稳定性及盆地大小；将该框架应用于非局部与全局连接网络，并考虑随机相位延迟情形。

Result: 发现网络中广泛存在多稳态性及盆地大小的强不对称性，揭示了传统稳定性分析无法探测的手性（chiral）动力学。

Conclusion: 复合矩阵的谱提供了从连接性与相位延迟直接估计锁相态盆地大小的解析途径，能够捕捉超越线性稳定性分析范围的复杂多稳态与手性动力学行为。

Abstract: We study how network connectivity and heterogeneous phase-delays shape the spatiotemporal dynamics of finite oscillator networks. Phase-delays can destabilize global synchronization and promote phase-locked patterns, including states with uniform phase gradients and more complex combinations of these modes. Yet, how connectivity and phase-delays jointly determine which states the network selects remains unclear. Here, we show that the spectrum of a composite matrix, which combines connectivity and phase-delays, governs not only the linear stability of the network's collective states but also their basin sizes. This, in turn, enables analytical estimates of basin size of phase-locked states for individual networks from connectivity and phase-delays alone. Applying this framework to nonlocal and global networks, including cases with random phase-delays, we uncover multistability and strong asymmetries in basin sizes, revealing chiral dynamics that conventional stability analysis cannot detect.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: McKean在1966年提出了一个长期悬而未决的问题：Boltzmann方程的熵产生是否必须随时间单调递减。这一问题对理解Boltzmann方程趋向平衡态的动力学行为具有根本性意义，但此前一直缺乏确定的回答，尤其是缺乏严谨的反例构造。

Method: 作者在空间均匀设定下，针对具有恒定角截面的Boltzmann碰撞算子，考察动能参数γ∈[0,1]的情形（覆盖硬球和Maxwell分子）。通过构造径向对称的Maxwell分布混合体作为具体反例，验证熵产生函数并不单调递减。

Result: 作者成功构造了使熵产生不单调递减的显式反例，从而否定了McKean猜想在该设定下的成立性。该结果不仅适用于Maxwell分子（γ=0），也适用于包括硬球（γ=1）在内的更一般情形。

Conclusion: McKean关于Boltzmann方程熵产生单调递减的猜想在空间均匀、恒定角截面情形下被推翻。熵的H定理保证熵本身趋于平衡，但熵产生率的单调性并非Boltzmann动力学的一般性质，这对动力论方程的松弛过程研究提供了新的认识。

Abstract: In 1966, McKean asked whether the entropy production of the Boltzmann equation must be monotone decreasing in time. We show that this is not the case even in the space-homogeneous setting for the Boltzmann collision operator with a constant angular cross section and the kinetic parameter $γ\in [0,1]$. This recovers the classical case of hard spheres and the simplest case of Maxwell molecules. Our examples are radially symmetric mixtures of Maxwellians.

</details>


### [83] [An extension theory for fully fractional Schrödinger equations with memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01826)
*Nicola Garofalo,Gigliola Staffilani*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究带记忆的非线性Schrödinger方程，通过完全分数阶算子(∂_t-iΔ_x)^s (0<s<1)的扩展理论，构造了半群定义、等价局部扩展问题和振荡Poisson核，建立了历史数据的Poisson提升与非局部能量之间的恒等关系，并在扩展诱导的温和意义下建立了关联非线性记忆问题的适定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于算子(∂_t-iΔ_x)^s在时间和空间上都是非局部的，经典Cauchy问题的初始条件不再适用，自然的历史设置（prescribed past history）要求建立新的数学框架。同时，算子同时非局部于时空，缺乏可直接利用的适定性理论和能量结构，需要发展扩展理论以分析该非线性记忆问题。

Method: (1) 构造(∂_t-iΔ_x)^s的半群定义；(2) 推导在额外空间变量上的等价局部扩展问题；(3) 显式计算相应的振荡Poisson核；(4) 通过扩展建立历史数据的Poisson提升，引入关于历史的固有二次形式，证明加权体能量与非局部能量之间的显式恒等式；(5) 结合伴论文中的非线性边界相互作用理论，建立非线性记忆问题的适定性理论，其中适定性为扩展诱导的温和意义，且附加图域假设下证明分数阶方程本身在L^2和正时间下的恢复。

Result: 建立了(∂_t-iΔ_x)^s的半群定义和等价局部扩展问题，显式给出振荡Poisson核；证明了扩展提升的加权体能量与完全由历史演化决定的非局部能量之间的恒等式；在扩展诱导的温和意义下获得了非线性记忆问题的适定性，并在图域假设下恢复了L^2意义下的分数阶方程（对正时间成立）。

Conclusion: 该工作为时空完全非局部的分数阶Schrödinger算子的记忆问题提供了严格的扩展理论基础，建立了历史数据到解的Poisson提升框架和能量恒等式，由此在温和意义下解决了非线性记忆问题的适定性，为进一步研究一般历史空间中的非线性现象提供了数学基础。

Abstract: We study nonlinear Schrödinger equations with memory through an extension theory for the fully fractional Schrödinger operator \[ (\partial_t-iΔ_x)^s,\qquad 0<s<1. \] Because the operator is nonlocal in both space and time, its natural Cauchy problem is posed with a prescribed past history rather than a classical initial condition. We construct a semigroup definition of $(\partial_t-iΔ_x)^s$, derive an equivalent local extension problem in one additional spatial variable, and compute the corresponding oscillatory Poisson kernel explicitly. The extension yields a Poisson lifting of history data and an intrinsic quadratic form on histories, establishing an explicit identity between the weighted bulk energy of the lifting and a nonlocal energy determined entirely by the prescribed past evolution. As an application, and in combination with the nonlinear boundary-interaction theory developed in our companion paper, we obtain a well-posedness theory for the associated nonlinear memory problem. The well-posedness is established in an extension-induced mild sense: for a general history in the natural energy space the future evolution is defined as the boundary trace of the corresponding solution in the half-space, and the fractional equation itself is recovered, in $L^2$ and for positive times, under an additional graph-domain hypothesis.

</details>

Conclusion: 乘积型洛伦兹流形上具有周期时间的方程整体上保持亚椭圆性，但在质量参数或时间周期落入特定小测度集合时正则性估计会退化，这一现象与特征值计数函数的渐近行为密切相关。

Abstract: We study the hypoellipticity of operators on a product type Lorentzian manifold where the time variable is periodic. In particular, we prove that hypoellipticity holds for such time-periodic equations, with a stronger estimate when the mass parameter or time period lies outside a set of arbitrarily small measure. We consider both compact and noncompact spatial manifolds and provide explicit examples involving the wave operator. The proof relies on a Fourier series decomposition and asymptotics for eigenvalue counting functions.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在半经典散射理论中，逆散射问题旨在从观测数据（如散射矩阵）反演未知势能或度量。固定能量的逆散射问题在数学物理和地球物理、声学等领域有广泛应用。本文动机在于弥合半经典（量子）散射信息与经典散射信息之间的鸿沟，探究在h→0的极限下，量子的半经典散射矩阵是否足以恢复经典散射映射乃至势能本身。

Method: 本文采用多层次的证明方法：(1) 利用Ingremeau对散射矩阵在相干态上作用的精确渐近描述，将散射矩阵的o(1)差转化为经典散射映射的等价关系；(2) 应用Stefanov–Uhlmann–Vasy的经典刚性定理，在virial条件与能量高于势能的假设下证明势能唯一性；(3) 对径向短程排斥势，结合Robert–Tamura的半经典散射渐近理论，利用Firsov–Abel反演公式从单一固定能量下的微分散射截面恢复势能；(4) 将类似刚性论证推广到紧支撑度量扰动的场景。

Conclusion: 本文建立了半经典散射矩阵极限行为与经典散射映射之间严格的对应关系，为从固定能量的散射数据反演势能（及度量扰动）提供了新的理论框架和唯一性保证，拓展了逆散射理论在径向排斥势与度量扰动情形的适用性。

Abstract: We investigate inverse scattering at a fixed energy for semiclassical
  Schrödinger operators with smooth potentials. For compactly supported
  potentials, we show that, at a non-trapping energy, if the corresponding
  semiclassical scattering matrices differ by $o(1)$ in operator norm as
  $h\to0$, then the associated classical scattering maps coincide. The
  proof relies on Ingremeau's description of the action of the scattering
  matrix on coherent states. Combined with a classical rigidity result of
  Stefanov--Uhlmann--Vasy, this yields uniqueness of the potential under a
  natural virial condition, provided the energy lies above the potential.
  We then consider radial short-range repulsive potentials. Under a
  monotonicity assumption on the radial force, the classical scattering
  relation has a single branch. Combining the semiclassical scattering
  asymptotics of Robert--Tamura with the classical Firsov--Abel inversion
  formula, we show that the differential cross section at a single fixed
  energy determines the potential throughout the classically accessible
  region. Finally, we obtain an analogous rigidity result for simple
  compactly supported metric perturbations.

</details>

Abstract: In this work we prove a Payne-Weinberger type inequality for quantum dot Dirac operators defined on bounded and simply connected planar domains. This is a sharp upper bound for their first positive eigenvalue depending only on the isoperimetric deficit of the domain. To this end, we use a recently studied connection with the so-called $\overline\partial$-Robin Laplacian and we establish an analogous inequality for its first eigenvalue, relying on the corresponding inequality for the Robin Laplacian.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 在初始速度满足平均符号（averaged sign）条件下，证明了2<p≤10/3时解的有限时间爆破；与已有的p>10/3全局存在性和1<p≤3爆破结果相结合，确定了临界指数p_crit=10/3。

Conclusion: 二维半线性强阻尼波动方程关于幂非线性|u|^p的临界指数被完整确定为10/3，这为理解强阻尼效应对非线性波动方程临界行为的影响提供了完备的理论刻画。

Abstract: In this manuscript, we determine the critical exponent for the two-dimensional semilinear wave equation with strong damping. A recent result in D'Abbicco (arXiv, 2026) gives global in-time small data solutions for $p>\frac{10}{3}$, whereas we in the paper prove finite-time blow-up for $2<p\leqslant\frac{10}{3}$ under an averaged sign condition on the initial velocity. Together with the previously known blow-up result for $1<p\leqslant3$, the threshold for the power nonlinearity $|u|^p$ is \begin{align*}
  p=p_{\mathrm{crit}}=\frac{10}{3}. \end{align*} We prove positivity of the full two-dimensional velocity fundamental solution and construct a positive averaged kernel, which allows us to establish a nonlinear lower-bound iteration in a parabolic region.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: (1) 在Sobolev空间中建立局部适定性理论：利用随机偏微分方程的标准固定点论证和亚临界正则性估计证明极大经典解的存在唯一性，并推导爆破判据；(2) 对Stratonovich输运噪声的特殊情形，结合初始非局部陡度的大条件，利用概率估计和极大解的路径性质证明有限时间爆破具有任意高概率并给出寿命的显式上界；(3) 在运输极大点处非局部陡度发散的事件上，建立条件I型爆破上界；当归一化非局部能量的终端Cesàro平均收敛时，通过极限值刻画精确的首阶爆破速率。

Result: (1) 证明了对一类乘性Stratonovich噪声（振幅为近斜自伴伪微分算子）驱动的随机CCF方程，Sobolev空间中极大经典解局部存在且唯一，并得到爆破判据；(2) 对Stratonovich输运噪声，当初始非局部陡度充分大时，以任意高概率发生有限时间爆破，且得到显式寿命上界；(3) 在非局部陡度发散事件上获得条件I型爆破上界，并在Cesàro平均收敛假设下确定精确的首阶爆破速率。

Conclusion: 本文首次系统建立了随机CCF方程在乘性Stratonovich噪声下的爆破理论，表明在足够大的初始数据下输运型噪声不能阻止有限时间爆破，且可给出精确的爆破速率刻画。该工作将确定性非局部输运方程的爆破机制推广到随机框架，为理解噪声对奇异形成动力学的影响提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: We study the stochastic Córdoba--Córdoba--Fontelos equation driven by multiplicative Stratonovich noise. The noise amplitude is allowed to be a pseudo-differential operator whose leading part is nearly skew-adjoint. This class contains classical transport noise and also permits genuinely nonlocal perturbations. We first develop a local-in-time theory for maximal classical solutions in Sobolev spaces, proving existence, uniqueness, and a blow-up criterion. We then consider the special case of Stratonovich transport. For sufficiently large initial nonlocal steepness at a global maximum, we prove finite-time blow-up with arbitrarily high prescribed probability and obtain an explicit upper bound on the lifespan. Finally, on the event that the nonlocal steepness at the transported maximum diverges, we establish a conditional Type-I upper bound. When the terminal Cesàro average of the normalised nonlocal energy converges, we further identify the exact leading-order blow-up rate in terms of its limiting value.

</details>

Abstract: In this paper, we study the large time behavior of solutions to the linearized Hartree equation near a stable equilibrium which is homogeneous in space and investigate the quantum Landau damping. Abstract: We prove existence of closed non-planar $p$-elasticae for general exponents $p\in (1,\infty)$. In particular, we show that for any $p \in (1,\infty)$, there exists a countable family of non-planar $p$-elasticae, which are realized as torus knots. This generalizes well-known results of Langer--Singer for the quadratic case $p=2$ to general exponents $p\in (1,\infty)$.

</details>


### [91] [Time-asymptotic stability of generic Riemann solutions for the system of heat-conductive ideal gas without viscosity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02119)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对于耗散仅作用于单个变量的部分耗散双曲-抛物系统，当激波和稀疏波同时存在时，其Riemann解的稳定性分析具有内在困难。特别是系统中缺乏速度耗散，使得处理与密度和速度相关的项时面临新的本质性挑战，需要建立相应的时间渐近稳定性理论。

Method: 受Kang-Vasseur-Wang近期工作的启发，本文通过以下方式克服困难：(1) 利用控制方程的精确结构和激波的附加性质；(2) 利用无粘性系统的波结构；(3) 对密度和速度分别进行空间-时间估计，以解决缺乏速度耗散引入的困难。

Result: 证明了无粘热传导理想气体系统解在时间趋于无穷时，均匀收敛到由稀疏波、粘性接触波和粘性激波（带时变位移）组成的复合波。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一维无粘热传导理想气体系统经典Riemann解（由激波、接触间断和稀疏波组成）的时间渐近稳定性，弥补了部分耗散系统中耗散仅作用于单变量情形下激波-稀疏波复合波稳定性的理论空白。

Abstract: This paper is concerned with the time-asymptotic stability of the generic Riemann solution for the one-dimensional system of heat-conductive ideal gas without viscosity, where the generic Riemann solution consists of a shock, a contact discontinuity, and a rarefaction wave. We prove that, as time tends to infinity, the solution of the non-viscous and heat-conductive ideal gas system converges uniformly to a composite wave composed of rarefaction wave, viscous contact wave, and viscous shock wave with a time-dependent shift. Motivated by the recent work of Kang-Vasseur-Wang [Arch. Ration. Mech. Anal. 249: 42 (2025)], we overcome the difficulties arising from the concurrence of shock and rarefaction waves for the partially dissipative hyperbolic-parabolic system with dissipation acting only on a single variable. More notably, the absence of velocity dissipation gives rise to new and intrinsic difficulties when handling the terms associated with the density and velocity. To resolve this, we exploit the precise structure of the governing equations and the additional properties of shock waves. Furthermore, we utilize the wave structure of the system without viscosity and perform separate space-time estimates for the density and velocity.

</details>


### [92] [Normalized solutions of $L^2$-supercritical NLS equations with periodic potentials and localized nonlinearities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02147)
*Zhentao He,Norihisa Ikoma,Chao Ji*

TL;DR: 本文研究带周期势的L²-超临界非线性薛定谔方程在范数约束下归一化解的存在性，证明了对所有足够小的μ>0存在归一化解，且当f在原点附近也具质量超临界增长时，该结论可扩展到所有μ>0。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 归一化解问题（即固定L²范数）在非线性薛定谔方程研究中具有重要意义，尤其在物理上对应粒子数守恒系统。已有结果大多关注于无势或势为常数/无界的情形，而对具有周期势且质量超临界的方程研究较少。本文旨在填补这一空白，在周期势背景下建立归一化解的存在性理论。

Method: 结合单调性技巧（monotonicity trick）、带Morse指标信息的约束泛函极小极大原理以及爆破分析（blow-up analysis）三种方法。通过构造合适的约束泛函，利用单调性插入参数化方法处理质量超临界带来的紧性缺失问题，再借助Morse指标信息建立临界点的结构，最终通过爆破分析处理极限情形。

Result: 证明了对所有足够小的μ>0，方程存在正的归一化解；当非线性项f在原点附近也呈质量超临界增长时，存在性可推广到任意μ>0的情形。

Conclusion: 在带1-周期势和局部非线性（由特征函数χ_Ω截断）的L²-超临界NLS方程中，只要势满足一般周期性和光滑性条件，归一化解在小质量情形下总是存在的；在额外假设下可消除μ的限制，获得对所有质量的完整存在性结果。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the existence of normalized solutions to the following $L^2$-supercritical nonlinear Schrödinger equation with a periodic potential \[
  \begin{dcases}
  -Δu +V (x)u + λu=χ_{Ω}(x)f(u)\quad \text{in }\mathbb{R}^N,
  u>0 \quad \text{in} \ \mathbb{R}^N,
  \int_{\mathbb{R}^N}\abs{u}^2\, dx =μ, \end{dcases} \] where $N \geq 1$, $μ>0$ is prescribed, $λ\in \mathbb{R}$ is a Lagrange multiplier, $V\in C(\mathbb{R}^N)$ is $1$-periodic in $x_1,...,x_N$, $f \in C^1(\mathbb{R})$ exhibits a general mass supercritical growth at infinity, $Ω\subset \mathbb{R}^N$ is a (nonempty) bounded open set with smooth boundary $\partial Ω$ and $χ_{Ω}$ is the characteristic function of $Ω$. We prove the existence of normalized solutions for all $μ>0$ sufficiently small. Moreover, if $f$ further has a mass-supercritical growth near the origin, then the existence result extends to every $μ>0$. The result is obtained through a combination of the monotonicity trick, minimax principle with Morse index information for constrained functionals and blow-up analysis.

</details>

Abstract: In this paper, we develop the method of moving planes entirely in the weak formulation for the fractional $p$-Laplacian in the singular range $1<p\leq2$. We first establish a small region principle for antisymmetric functions and apply it to prove radial symmetry and monotonicity of nonnegative weak solutions of fractional $p$-Laplacian equations in a bounded domain. We also consider the nonlocal quasilinear Lane--Emden equation $(-Δ)^s_pu=u^q$ in $\mathbb R^n$. In the Sobolev critical case, we establish radial symmetry, monotonicity, and precise asymptotic behavior at infinity for finite-energy weak solutions. Under a suitable decay condition, we also obtain radial symmetry for the full range $q>p-1$. Our results complete those of Chen-Li (Adv. Math., 2018: 735-758), where analogous results were obtained for $C^{1,1}$ solutions in the pointwise sense.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的伪微分算子理论中，不同的量子化方案（如Weyl、anti-Wick等）各自有独立的符号演算框架，缺乏统一的处理方式。作者希望建立一种更一般的微积分框架，使各种已知量子化成为其特例，从而在统一视角下研究符号类、有界性条件和复合律等核心问题，特别是探索哪些符号类在不同量子化下具有普适性。

Method: 作者以相空间$\mathbb{R}^{2n}$上的有界测度作为参数来定义新的伪微分微积分体系，每种测度诱导一种特定量子化。在此基础上：(1) 考察Gevrey类符号（参数1/2）作为所有量子化共通符号类的普适性；(2) 对测度施加精确的额外假设以允许更大的$L^{\infty}$符号类；(3) 利用Lévy-Khintchine公式证明Lévy过程概率测度（扩散系数大于1/4）满足该假设；(4) 给出一般量子化下两个符号复合的精确公式；(5) 以Wick量子化为基础，推导Mizrahi级数展开及算子与符号类之间的Banach代数同构。

Result: 研究表明Gevrey参数1/2类符号对所有一般微积分均成立，与测度选择无关。但对于更大的$L^{\infty}$符号类，需要测度满足特定的额外条件，该条件对anti-Wick量化成立但对Weyl量化不成立（Weyl情形恢复到Sjöstrand和Gröchenig的标准符号类）。扩散系数大于1/4的Lévy过程概率测度天然满足这些额外条件。此外，作者在一般框架下给出了符号复合公式，并建立了基于Wick量子化的Mizrahi级数展开与Banach代数同构结果。

Conclusion: 该文建立了一个以有界测度为参数、统一涵盖多种已知量子化的伪微分算子推广框架，揭示了Gevrey参数1/2类符号的普适性与$L^{\infty}$符号类所需测度条件的非平凡性，并通过Lévy过程建立了与概率论的深层联系。复合律、Mizrahi级数和Banach代数结构的结果为该理论在调和分析、谱理论与量子力学中的应用提供了统一工具。

Abstract: This article is concerned with generalizations of pseudo-differential operators in $L^2(\mathbb{R}^n)$, $n\geq 1$. The definition of the new calculi depends only on bounded measures on the phase space $\mathbb{R}^{2n}$ and each measure gives rise to a specific calculus. Quantizations of anti-Wick, Weyl, classical and Born-Jordan are particular cases of the general calculi. Classes of symbols in this framework are then studied. The Gevrey class of parameter 1/2 is a class of symbol that is common to all the general calculi, that is, a class of symbols independent on the bounded measures parametrizing the quantizations. Precise additional hypotheses on the measures are necessary in the aim to consider the larger class of symbols $L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{2n})$. This result can be applied for anti-Wick but not for Weyl quantization. Concerning Weyl pseudo-differential calculus, we recover the standard class of Sjöstrand and Gröchening. Then, we prove that probability measures of Lévy processes on the phase space $\mathbb{R}^{2n}$ with diffusion larger than 1/4 are natural examples of measures satisfying the latter additional hypotheses in order to consider $L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{2n})$ symbols. This relation is derived using the Lévy-Khintchine formula. Composition laws in that general context are next investigated. In that purpose, we give a formula for the composition of two symbols in some precise class of symbols valid for all general quantizations. General calculi are relying on Wick quantization which is therefore primarily examined for some precise classes of symbols. Additional results in that context are provided, such as Mizrahi series expansions and Banach algebra isomorphisms between operators and symbol classes.

</details>

Abstract: We extend the global existence results for critical parameters of [Bianchini and Leccese, J. Math. Anal. Appl. (2024)] to the multidimensional problem \begin{equation*}
  \partial_t u + \text{div } (b(t,x) u^{1+k}) = Δu \end{equation*} for $b:\mathbb R\times{\mathbb R}^d\to{\mathbb R}^d$ non-autonomous fields, in the case \begin{equation*}
  (\text{div } b)_-\in L^\infty_{\mathrm{loc}}
  \big([0,\infty);L^{p,\infty}({\mathbb R}^d)\big),
  \qquad p>d. \end{equation*} The proof of global existence follows the parabolic symmetrization approach of [Bandle, J. Analyse Math. (1976)] and is based on the study of the function \begin{equation*}
  m(t,s)=\int_0^s u^*(t,r)\,dr. \end{equation*}

### [96] [Viscosity Supersolution Barriers to a Non-local Free Boundary Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02381)
*Avetik Arakelyan,Lusine Poghosyan*

TL;DR: 本文研究带动态移动双边自由边界的抛物型障碍偏积分微分方程（PIDE），该问题源于含Lévy跳跃过程的投机性资产泡沫建模。文章在三个参数区间内探讨了具有线性渐近增长$O(|g|)$的粘性上解的存在性，并给出了关键的临界时间阈值判定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该问题来源于投机性资产泡沫的数学建模（含Lévy跳跃过程），需要为动态双边自由边界PIDE构建合适的粘性上解障碍，以确定解的存在性与稳定性边界。

Method: 作者通过分析稳定化局部漂移（由贴现率$r$与均值回归系数$\rho$决定）与非线性跳跃色散（由大跳跃强度$\lambda$与Lipschitz常数$L_\gamma$决定）之间的平衡关系，将问题划分为三种参数状态进行分析。利用渐近斜率包络（asymptotic slope envelope）方法推导临界时间阈值，并在边界情形通过构造光滑上解并附加空间无交叉条件来证明全局存在性。

Result: （1）当$r+\rho>\sqrt{\lambda L_\gamma}$时，证明非负粘性上解的全局存在性；（2）当$r+\rho<\sqrt{\lambda L_\gamma}$（赤字情形）时，证明有限时域存在性并推导出临界时间阈值$T_{\mathrm{crit}}$，且通过渐近斜率包络证明在$T_{\mathrm{crit}}$之后不存在线性增长的非负上解；（3）当$r+\rho=\sqrt{\lambda L_\gamma}$（临界情形）时，在附加空间无交叉条件下通过构造光滑上解证明全局存在性。

Conclusion: 粘性上解的存在性取决于局部漂移强度（$r+\rho$）与非局部跳跃色散强度（$\sqrt{\lambda L_\gamma}$）之间的竞争关系：仅当前者足够大时解可全局存在；在赤字情形下存在有限时间障碍；在精确临界点处解的存在依赖于额外的空间结构条件。

Abstract: We study a parabolic obstacle partial integro-differential equation (PIDE) with a dynamically moving bilateral free boundary. This type of problem arises in the mathematical modeling of speculative asset bubbles with Lévy jump processes. We consider the existence of viscosity supersolutions within the class of functions exhibiting linear asymptotic growth ($O(|g|)$ at infinity) across three distinct parametric regimes. Our intention is to determine when such a supersolution barrier can be built by analyzing the balance between the stabilizing local drift, defined by the discount rate $r$ and mean-reversion $ρ$, and the non-local jump dispersion, characterized by the large-jump intensity $λ$ and Lipschitz constant $L_γ$. First, when $r+ρ> \sqrtλL_γ$, we prove the global existence of non-negative viscosity supersolutions. Second, in the deficit regime ($r+ρ< \sqrtλL_γ$), we prove existence on finite horizons and derive a critical horizon threshold $T_{\mathrm{crit}}$. Utilizing an asymptotic slope envelope, we prove that no non-negative linear-growth supersolution can exist beyond $T_{\mathrm{crit}}$. Finally, at the exact critical boundary ($r+ρ= \sqrtλL_γ$), we show global existence by constructing a smooth supersolution, provided an additional spatial no-crossing condition holds.

</details>


### [97] [Kinetic Fokker--Planck Equations with Drifts in a Supercritical Range](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02385)
*Zikai Chen,Chongyang Ren*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动力学Fokker-Planck方程在等离子体物理和动理学理论中具有重要地位。当漂移项仅具有粗糙（低正则）的无散度结构时，经典方法难以处理；同时，在scaling-supercritical（超临界）范围内，方程的非线性或粗糙性使得解的全局正则性和适定性成为开放问题。作者希望在尽可能弱的漂移正则性假设下恢复解的有界性与全局适定性。

Method: (1) 利用漂移项的无散度（divergence-free）结构，证明在动力学局部化（kinetic localization）下的能量抵消（energy cancellation）性质仍然成立；(2) 结合各向异性（anisotropic）混合Lebesgue空间中的正则化估计；(3) 应用De Giorgi迭代方法，从弱次解的局部能量估计出发，逐级提升解的Lp可积性，最终得到局部有界性；(4) 由局部有界性推广到全局，建立Cauchy问题的整体适定性。

Result: 在各项异性混合Lebesgue空间的粗糙无散度漂移假设下，证明了动力学Fokker-Planck方程弱次解的局部有界性；并在此结果基础上，建立了scaling-supercritical区域中Cauchy问题的全局适定性（存在性与唯一性等）。

Conclusion: 无散度结构所提供的能量抵消在动力学框架的局部化过程中依然稳健，与各向异性正则化估计和De Giorgi迭代相结合，可在超临界范围内克服漂移项的粗糙性，为动力学Fokker-Planck方程获得弱解的局部有界性及全局适定性。

Abstract: We investigate kinetic Fokker--Planck equations with rough divergence-free drifts in anisotropic mixed Lebesgue spaces. The divergence-free structure yields an energy cancellation that remains effective under kinetic localization. Combining this cancellation with anisotropic regularization estimates and a De Giorgi iteration, we establish local boundedness for weak subsolutions and, consequently, global well-posedness for the Cauchy problem in a scaling-supercritical regime.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非自伴的非局部扩散主特征值问题缺乏变分刻画，漂移项的引入进一步破坏了原有的二次变分结构（如Berestycki-Coville-Vo在无漂移对称问题中所利用的），并在有界区间上引入单侧流入-流出边界几何，使得传统的变分论证失效，需要发展新方法。

Conclusion: 本文通过一系列直接估计替代了缺失的变分论证，成功处理了非自伴非局部主特征值问题中的核心技术难点，为漂移驱动的非局部扩散算子提供了完整的定性与定量分析框架。

Abstract: We consider generalized principal eigenvalues of one-dimensional nonlocal dispersal operators with a drift of fixed sign. A principal difficulty in this non-self-adjoint setting is the absence of a variational characterization of the principal value. In the symmetric drift-free problem, the critical nonlocal-to-local limit can be treated through a quadratic variational structure and Sobolev-seminorm approximation as Berestycki-Coville-Vo \cite{BCV}. The drift destroys this structure and, on a bounded interval, introduces at the same time a one-sided inflow--outflow boundary geometry. Replacing the missing variational argument by estimates which remain stable under singular rescaling is therefore a central technical issue. Our approach is direct : we work with a single generalized principal value and prove the maximum principle, simplicity in the positive cone and the scaling limits from the equation itself, without passing through auxiliary generalized principal eigenvalues analogous to $λ_1'$ and $λ_1''$ in Berestycki--Rossi \cite{BR}. The replacement mechanisms are directional Harnack inequalities and coefficient barriers, direct--adjoint identities, logarithmic Collatz--Wielandt transforms, Fourier coercivity of zero extensions and scale-dependent localization. At the critical diffusive scale, Fourier compactness and an exact energy--transport identity recover the missing inflow Dirichlet condition and identify the local Dirichlet limit without a Rayleigh quotient. For variable coefficients we determine the complete small-range phase diagram on bounded intervals and on the line, and we obtain sharp large-range three-term asymptotics by a rank-one reduction and a corner Laplace analysis in the advective travel-time variable. In the homogeneous whole-line problem the critical correction is of order $σ^2$.

</details>


### [99] [On the local well-posedness of the Benjamin-Ono-Zakharov-Kuznetsov equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02423)
*Ailton C. Nascimento*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究R²上Benjamin–Ono–Zakharov–Kuznetsov（BO–ZK）方程的柯西问题，通过建立新的极大函数估计并实施Kenig–Koenig方法，将局部适定性阈值从s>5/4降至s>19/16，优于Nascimento(2020)的已知各向同性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在改进BO–ZK方程在R²上的已知局部适定性结果。此前最佳各向同性结果（Nascimento 2020）要求s>5/4，作者希望利用Kenig–Koenig框架降低该正则性阈值，并弥合与各向异性理论的差距。

Method: 遵循Kenig和Ziesler的策略，建立适应BO–ZK方程的新的极大函数估计，并以此实施Kenig–Koenig方法。核心工具包括：锐利的二adic混合极大函数估计、利用纵/横向群速度互补行为的各向异性局部光滑机制（横向色散补偿特征区域附近的纵向光滑退化）、精细的短时Strichartz估计，以及修正能量论证。

Result: 将局部适定性阈值从s>5/4改进到s>19/16。在BO–ZK端点处，各向同性数据类包含先前理论的各向异性E^{5/4+}类。在H^s(R²)的有界子集上，解的存在时间满足T≳_s(1+‖u₀‖_{H^s})^{-8}。

Conclusion: 在相应解类中建立了存在性、唯一性和对初始数据的连续依赖性。所得到的阈值反映了当前方法的优化程度，并明确声明不声称其为最优结果（sharp）。

Abstract: We study the Cauchy problem for the Benjamin--Ono--Zakharov--Kuznetsov equation on $\mathbb R^2$. Following the strategy of Kenig and Ziesler, we establish new maximal-function estimates adapted to the BO--ZK equation and use them to implement the Kenig--Koenig method. As a result, we improve the best previously known isotropic result of Nascimento (2020), lowering the local well-posedness threshold from $s>5/4$ to $s>19/16$. At the BO--ZK endpoint, the resulting isotropic data class also contains the anisotropic $E^{5/4+}$ class of the preceding theory. On bounded subsets of $H^s(\mathbb R^2)$, the lifespan may be chosen so that $
  T\gtrsim_s \bigl(1+\norm{u_0}_{H^s}\bigr)^{-8}. $
  The proof combines a sharp dyadic mixed maximal-function estimate with an anisotropic local-smoothing mechanism that exploits the complementary behavior of the longitudinal and transverse group velocities. In particular, transverse dispersion compensates for the degeneration of longitudinal smoothing near the characteristic region. Together with refined short-time Strichartz estimates and a modified energy argument, these ingredients close the nonlinear estimates at the stated regularity. Existence, uniqueness, and continuous dependence on the initial data are then established in the corresponding solution class. The resulting threshold reflects the present optimization of the method and is not claimed to be sharp.

</details>


### [100] [Power convexity of the torsion function on horo-convex domains in hyperbolic space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02516)
*Wei Zhang,Qi Zhou*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了双曲空间中边界光滑且严格horoconvex有界区域上的扭转问题，证明了当区域直径足够小时，$-\sqrt{u}$是严格凸的（其中$u$为扭转函数），并证明了指数$1/2$的最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在双曲空间中研究扭转函数$u$的平方根凸性是几何分析中的一个重要问题。与欧氏空间中的结果相类比，作者希望探索双曲空间中该性质是否依然成立，以及成立所需的条件。

Method: 证明的三大核心工具包括：常数秩定理（constant rank theorem）、边界凸性估计（boundary convexity estimate），以及区域的形变论证（deformation argument）。通过这些工具的组合，作者逐步证明了$-\sqrt{u}$的严格凸性。

Result: 本文证明了在双曲空间中，对于直径足够小的光滑严格horoconvex有界区域，$-\sqrt{u}$是严格凸的。此外，作者通过构造反例（即使在任意小直径的严格horoconvex区域中）证明了指数$1/2$是最优的，即不能改小。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了双曲空间中扭转函数平方根凸性的一个充分条件（小直径），同时揭示了指数$1/2$在该框架下的最优性，深化了对双曲空间中椭圆方程解几何性质的理解。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the torsion problem on bounded, smooth, strictly horo-convex domains in hyperbolic space. We prove that if the diameter of the domain is sufficiently small, then $-\sqrt{u}$ is strictly convex, where $u$ denotes the torsion function. The main ingredients of our proof are the constant rank theorem, a boundary convexity estimate, and a deformation argument for the domain. We also show that the exponent $1/2$ is optimal, even among strictly horo-convex domains of arbitrarily small diameter.

</details>


### [101] [Strong Ill-Posedness in critical and subcritical regimes for the Hunter-Saxton equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02523)
*Billel Guelmame,Haroune Houamed*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究实直线上的Hunter–Saxton方程在能量空间L∞∩Ḣ¹中的全局耗散解，证明了在Lipschitz阈值及以下的强不适定性：对每个s∈(1,3/2]，构造了初值u₀∈L∞∩Ḣ¹∩Ḣˢ，其唯一全局耗散解对任意T>0都不属于C([0,T];Ḣˢ)。临界与次临界区间的构造策略截然不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hunter–Saxton方程是一类重要的不可压缩流体模型（液晶向列相），其解在有限时间内可能失去正则性。作者旨在揭示该方程在能量空间中更精细的Sobolev正则性失效机制，明确临界值s=3/2处强不适定性的本质，填补关于该方程在Lipschitz阈值以下不适定性程度的空白。

Method: 构造性证明方法。对于次临界区间1<s<3/2，作者通过叠加具有递增负斜率的重标度化局部化"泡"（bubbles），利用次临界标度保持Ḣˢ可和性，再结合显式特征线公式产生范数膨胀。对于临界情形s=3/2，标度不提供小性，作者改用对数分布的紧支撑多尺度剖面，其破裂时间收敛到零，并借助单侧局部化原理和几乎正交性估计将单个剖面的膨胀传导给全局耗散解。

Result: 证明了Hunter–Saxton方程在能量空间中的强不适定性：对所有s∈(1,3/2]，存在上述正则类的初值，其唯一全局耗散解在任意时间区间内都不具备连续依赖的Ḣˢ正则性。次临界和临界情形分别通过不同的构造实现，其中临界情形s=3/2依赖更精细的多尺度分析与几乎正交性估计。

Conclusion: 该结果确立了Hunter–Saxton方程在能量空间中Sobolev正则性的瞬时失效，将不适定性阈值精确刻画在Lipschitz水平（即s=3/2之前），并表明临界情形需要本质上不同于次临界的构造方法，深化了对该方程弱解/耗散解正则性理论的完整认识。

Abstract: We study the Hunter--Saxton equation on the real line and its global dissipative solution in the energy space $
L^\infty(\mathbb R)\cap \dot H^1(\mathbb R). $
We prove strong ill-posedness through instantaneous failure of Sobolev regularity at and below the Lipschitz threshold. More precisely, for every $s\in(1,\nicefrac32]$, we construct $
u_0\in L^\infty(\mathbb R)\cap\dot H^1(\mathbb R)\cap\dot H^s(\mathbb R) $
whose unique global dissipative solution satisfies $u\notin C([0,T]; \dot H^s (\mathbb R))$, for every $T>0$.
  The constructions differ substantially in the subcritical and critical regimes. For $1<s<\nicefrac32$, we superpose rescaled, localized bubbles with increasingly negative slopes; subcritical scaling preserves $\dot H^s$-summability, while the explicit characteristic formula produces norm inflation. At $s=\nicefrac32$, where scaling yields no smallness, we use logarithmically distributed compactly supported multiscale profiles whose breaking times converge to zero. A one-sided localization principle and an almost-orthogonality estimate then transfer the inflation of individual profiles to the full dissipative solution.

</details>


### [102] [Resolvent Estimates for the Stokes Operator in a Three-Dimensional Lipschitz Domain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02552)
*Zhongwei Shen*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文通过改进已有方法，在三维有界Lipschitz域Ω中建立了Stokes算子的L^p预解估计（对所有1<p≤∞成立），进而证明Stokes算子在L^p_σ(Ω)上生成一致有界的解析半群。这一结果推翻了长期以来认为三维Lipschitz域中大p情形下L^p预解估计不成立的猜想。附录中证明了L^2_σ(Ω)与L^∞_σ(Ω)之间的插值定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期以来学界普遍认为在三维Lipschitz域中，Stokes算子的L^p预解估计可能对大的p失效，本文旨在挑战并推翻这一根深蒂固的认知，建立更广泛的L^p预解估计。

Method: 通过对AGH-2015和GS2026a中发展的方法进行精细化改进，利用L^2_σ(Ω)与L^∞_σ(Ω)之间的插值定理（在附录中给出证明），将预解估计从低指数情形推广至所有1<p≤∞。

Result: 成功建立了三维有界Lipschitz域中Stokes算子对任意1<p≤∞的L^p预解估计，并证明Stokes算子在L^p_σ(Ω)上生成一致有界解析半群，推翻了既有猜想。

Conclusion: 三维Lipschitz域中Stokes算子的L^p预解估计对所有p∈(1,∞]均成立，这一出乎意料的结果表明该问题在大p情形下并非不可克服，同时附录中的插值定理为后续研究提供了重要工具。

Abstract: By refining the approach developed in \cite{AGH-2015, GS2026a}, we establish $L^p$ resolvent estimates for the Stokes operator in a bounded Lipschitz domain $Ω$ in $\R^3$ for any $1< p\le \infty$. As a consequence, the Stokes operator generates a uniformly bounded analytic semigroup in $L^p_σ(Ω)$. The results are particularly surprising, as it is long believed that the $L^p$ resolvent estimates in three-dimensional Lipschitz domains may fail for large $p$. In the Appendix we prove a theorem on interpolation between $L^2_σ(Ω)$ and $L^\infty_σ(Ω)$.

</details>


### [103] [Interior Curvature Estimates for the Graphical Scalar Curvature Equation in All Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02581)
*Guohuan Qiu,Jin Yan*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文解决了图形标量曲率方程 admissible 解的内 C² 估计这一长期未解决问题，在所有维度 n≥3 上建立了内曲率估计。证明结合了 Jacobi 不等式、双曲面极大值原理和双曲面 Pogorelov 估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图形标量曲率方程 admissible 解的内 C² 估计在所有维度 n≥3 上的可证明性是一个长期悬而未决的问题，此前未能解决，需要新的技术手段来突破。

Method: 证明的核心方法是将三种工具相结合：Jacobi 不等式用于控制曲率的传播；双曲面极大值原理用于处理内点极值的约束；双曲面 Pogorelov 估计用于处理边界附近或退化情形下的先验界。

Result: 在所有维度 n≥3 上，成功证明了常图形标量曲率方程 admissible 解的内 C² 估计（即内曲率估计），填补了这一领域的空白。

Conclusion: 通过组合 Jacobi 不等式、双曲面极大值原理和双曲面 Pogorelov 估计三种工具，最终完整解决了所有维度下图形标量曲率方程的内 C² 估计问题，为后续研究该方程的解的正则性和收敛性提供了关键的分析基础。

Abstract: We resolve the long-standing problem of establishing interior \(C^2\) estimates for admissible solutions of the graphical scalar curvature equation in every dimension \(n\ge 3\). More precisely, we prove interior curvature estimates for admissible solutions to the constant graphical scalar curvature equation. The proof combines Jacobi inequalities with a two-surface maximum principle and a two-surface Pogorelov estimate.

</details>


### [104] [Spatial inhomogeneity for a three-dimensional doubly degenerate nutrient system with indirect consumption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02607)
*Ai Huang,Xiangmao De-ji,Jing Li,Yifu Wang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个具有间接消耗的三维双重退化营养趋化系统的全局动力学行为，证明了在合适初值下系统存在全局弱解，且该解在时间趋于无穷时收敛到平衡态。特别地，当初值u0非常数且v0质量足够小时，极限轮廓是非齐次的，揭示了营养匮乏环境中的图案形成，而关键工具是若干新颖下界泛函不等式的引入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双重退化（同时含uv∇u与u²v∇v）的营养趋化系统在三维空间中的全局动力学尚未被充分理解，特别是系统的长时间行为、收敛性以及在营养匮乏条件下的图案形成机制仍属开放问题。本研究旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 作者通过构造合适的逼近系统（正则化方法）并结合能量估计建立全局弱解的存在性；为了控制退化项的困难，创新性地引入了下界泛函不等式，对形如∫_Ω u^k v|∇u|²（k>-1）的积分给出估计，从而克服三维情形下系统的退化性与非线性耦合带来的关键技术障碍；最终通过建立能量函数（Lyapunov泛函）的收敛性论证解的长时间渐近行为。

Result: 对于满足适当正则性的初值(u0,v0,w0)，系统在三维有界域上存在全局弱解；该弱解在合适的拓扑意义下收敛到平衡态(u∞,0,w∞)；当u0非常数且v0质量足够小时，收敛极限(u∞,w∞)是空间非齐次的，表明营养匮乏环境下可以涌现非平凡的稳态图案。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了三维双重退化营养趋化系统全局弱解的长时间收敛理论，并揭示了非齐次稳态的存在性，其中引入的新颖泛函不等式是处理此类双重退化系统的主要分析工具，为后续相关模型的动力学研究提供了方法论基础。

Abstract: This paper investigates the global dynamics of a doubly degenerate nutrient-taxis system with indirect consumption: \begin{equation*} \left\{ \begin{aligned} &u_{t}=\nabla \cdot (uv\nabla u)-\nabla \cdot (u^{2}v\nabla v)+\ell vw,&x\in Ω,\, t>0,\\ & v_{t}=Δv-vw,&x\in Ω,\, t>0,\\ &w_t=Δw-w+u,&x\inΩ,t>0 \end{aligned} \right. \end{equation*} posed on a smooth bounded domain $Ω\subset\mathbb{R}^{3}$ with no-flux boundary conditions. It is shown that for suitably regular initial data $(u_0,v_0,w_0)$, the associated initial-boundary value problem admits a global weak solution. Furthermore, in an appropriate topological setting, this solution converges to an equilibrium $(u_\infty, 0,w_\infty)$ as $t\rightarrow \infty$. Notably, when $u_0$ is nonconstant and the mass of $v_0$ is sufficiently small, the limiting profiles $u_{\infty}$ and $w_{\infty}$ are are spatially nonhomogeneous, capturing emergent patterning in nutrient-depleted environments. A cornerstone of our analysis is the introduction of novel functional inequalities, which provide estimates from below for the integral $\int_Ωu^{k}v|\nabla u|^2$ with some $k>-1$.

</details>


### [105] [Degeneracy Set Comparison Principle and Free Boundary Estimates for Hénon-type Infinity Laplace equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02612)
*Yantian Chen,Feida Jiang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究由无穷Laplace算子驱动的Hénon型方程（含退化权、强吸收项和额外源项）的非负粘性解，关注退化集中的自由边界点。论文证明了退化集比较原理（在退化集上取值确定的分片内保证唯一性），建立了自由边界点附近的尖锐改进正则性估计（本征增长率为r^{(4+α)/(3-m)}），并获得了匹配的非退化估计，从而证明解以该速率脱离零相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hénon型方程结合无穷Laplacian、退化权和强吸收项后，自由边界问题的全局唯一性通常不成立，解在退化集中的行为及其与零相分离的速率缺乏系统研究。本文旨在填补这一理论空白，为带有退化权重的非线性椭圆方程自由边界理论提供新的刻画工具。

Method: 采用粘性解框架，通过建立退化集比较原理来处理全局唯一性缺失问题；利用改进的Bernstein-type技术或Pucci型极值原理推导自由边界附近的显式正则性上界估计；结合非退化估计（在非齐次情形下附加适定条件）与正则性估计，证明解以精确的本征增长率r^{(4+α)/(3-m)}脱离零相。

Result: （1）证明了退化集比较原理，在退化集取值固定的分片中解唯一，但说明全局唯一性一般不成立；（2）在自由边界点附近获得匹配的上下界估计，明确本征增长率为r^{(4+α)/(3-m)}；（3）非退化性质在非齐次情形下需附加特定条件方可成立，且该性质保证解在零相附近以同一速率分离。

Conclusion: 本文系统刻画了退化权重下无穷Laplacian型Hénon方程自由边界解的局部行为，确立了退化集中解的唯一性框架和精确的增长率，为后续研究含退化权的自由边界正则性问题提供了坚实的分析基础。

Abstract: In this work, we study nonnegative viscosity solutions to Hénon-type equations driven by the infinity-Laplacian with a degenerate weight, strong absorption and an additional source term. We focus on free boundary points lying in the degeneracy set of the weight. We prove the degeneracy set comparison principle, which yields uniqueness within each slice determined by the value on the degeneracy set of the weight, although global uniqueness is not available in general. We establish sharp improved regularity estimates near free boundary points, with the intrinsic growth rate $r^{\frac{4+α}{3-m}}$. Finally, we obtain a matching non-degeneracy estimate at free boundary points; the property holds in the inhomogeneous case with suitable condition. Consequently, solutions detach from their zero phase at this rate.

</details>


### [106] [Comparison principles and symmetry for subquadratic fractional $p$-Laplacian equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02626)
*Dong Ye,Weimin Zhang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文建立了亚二次分式p-Laplacian算子的比较原理框架，在最小正则性假设下证明了弱比较原理和强比较原理，并将其应用于移动平面法和解的对称性研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于算子$(-\Delta)_p^s$在$1<p<2$时具有奇异性，在最小正则性假设下建立比较原理一直是一个重大挑战性难题，而比较原理是移动平面法在该设定下所需的必要分析工具。先前研究严重依赖Hölder甚至$C^{1,1}$正则性，需要更弱假设下的结果。

Method: 首先在有界小测度区域中对$(-\Delta)_p^s u = f(u)$证明了弱比较原理，仅需弱解的有界性。其次引入了局部化障碍函数（localized barrier function）来建立强比较原理，适用于参数范围$s \in (0, \frac{1}{2})$和$\frac{1}{1-s} < p < 2$，不需要Hölder正则性或区域的光滑性要求。

Result: 在参数范围$s \in (0, \frac{1}{2})$和$\frac{1}{1-s} < p < 2$下，建立了连续弱解的强比较原理；在一般范围内建立了有界弱解的弱比较原理。作为直接应用，在温和假设下证明了弱解$(-\Delta)_p^s u = f(u)$的对称性。

Conclusion: 该框架显著拓展了非局部拟线性方程的对称性理论，为移动平面法在奇异亚二次分式p-Laplacian设定下的应用提供了完整的分析工具基础。

Abstract: This paper establishes a new comparison principle framework for the subquadratic fractional $p$-Laplacian, i.e.~$1 < p < 2$
  under minimal regularity assumptions, that has remained a significant challenging issue due to the singularity of the operator. Our results provide the essential analytical tools required for the moving plane method in this setting. We prove first a weak comparison principle for $(-Δ)_p^s u = f(u)$ in bounded domains with sufficiently small measure, where only the boundedness of the weak solution is required. More importantly, we establish a strong comparison principle for continuous weak solutions in the parameter range $s \in (0, \frac{1}{2})$ and $\frac{1}{1-s} < p < 2$. Our proof introduces a localized barrier function and does not require any Hölder regularity of the weak solution, nor any smoothness of the domain. This presents a substantial contrast over previous study, which relied heavily on Hölder or even $C^{1,1}$ regularity. As a direct application, we employ these comparison principles to prove the symmetry of weak solutions to $(-Δ)_p^s u = f(u)$ under mild assumptions, which significantly extend existing symmetry theories for nonlocal quasilinear equations.

</details>


### [107] [Conservation of mass for solutions of Leibenson's equation on Riemannian Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02732)
*Philipp Sürig*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了黎曼流形上的Leibenson方程（含p-Laplacian的非线性扩散方程）的质量守恒性质，给出了不同参数范围下的充分条件，并证明了双曲空间上有限熄灭时间从而质量不守恒，最后利用特殊情况得到部分Liouville性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黎曼流形上的非线性退化/奇异扩散方程（Leibenson方程）在物理和几何中具有重要意义。作者希望在线性/欧氏空间中已知结果的基础上，推广到一般黎曼流形，并探讨体积增长条件与质量守恒之间的精确联系。这一问题的动力学行为依赖于参数（p,q），需要精确刻画体积增长假定。此外，Liouville性质回答Holopainen提出的猜想也是重要动机。

Method: 通过体积增长假设分析Leibenson方程解的质量守恒。采用能量估计、比较原理、以及流形上热核/体积增长性质的精细分析。对于q(p-1)≥1的情形，利用指数型体积增长条件V(x0,r)≤exp(C r^{p/(p-1)})来建立守恒；对于q(p-1)<1的情形，利用幂次型体积增长条件V(x0,r)≤Cr^N并结合p>N[1-q(p-1)]条件。在双曲空间上利用其特殊几何结构构造/分析解的熄灭行为。利用q(p-1)=1时的守恒结果证明L^{p-1}-Liouville性质。

Result: (1)当q(p-1)≥1时，在体积指数增长V(x0,r)≤exp(C r^{p/(p-1)})下质量守恒；(2)当q(p-1)<1时，在V(x0,r)≤Cr^N且p>N[1-q(p-1)]下质量守恒，该阈值与R^n中n=N的情形匹配；(3)在双曲空间H^n上，q(p-1)<1时解具有有限熄灭时间，因而质量不守恒；(4)q(p-1)=1情形下的守恒结果导致L^{p-1}-Liouville性质，部分回答了Holopainen的猜想。

Conclusion: 本文给出了黎曼流形上Leibenson方程质量守恒的充分条件，揭示了体积增长与参数范围之间的精确相互作用，并通过双曲空间的熄灭现象表明该条件在某种意义上是本质的。其结果部分证实了Holopainen的猜想。

Abstract: We consider on a Riemannian manifold $M$ the Leibenson equation \begin{equation*}\label{eqabs}\partial _{t}u=Δ_{p}u^{q},\end{equation*} where $p>1$ and $q>0$. When $q(p-1)\geq 1$, we prove conservation of mass for solutions of Leibenson's equation assuming only the volume bound $V(x_0, r)\leq \exp\left(C r^{\frac{p}{p-1}}\right)$ for some $x_{0}\in M$ and all large enough $r>0$. When $q(p-1)< 1$, we prove this property assuming $V(x_0, r)\leq Cr^{N}$ and $p>N[1-q(p-1)]$, which matches the threshold in $\mathbb{R}^{n}$ with $N=n$. We also show that solutions on the hyperbolic space $\mathbb{H}^{n}$ have a finite extinction time in the case $q(p-1)< 1$, which implies that the conservation of mass property does not hold. Using the conservation of mass result in the case $q(p-1)=1$, we also prove a $L^{p-1}$- Liouville property, which partially answers a conjecture stated by I. Holopainen \cite{holopainen2000sharp}.

</details>


### [108] [Divergence-Free Approximation in Sobolev and Lebesgue Spaces on General Unbounded Domains with Applications to Energy Equality in Fluid Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02769)
*Akram Khan,Sagar Gautam,Manil T. Mohan*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文构造了无散度的向量值逼近函数，适用于全空间R^d（d>=2）以及一般的C^{1,1}型无界域。这些逼近在Sobolev和Lebesgue空间中同时收敛。在全空间情形下利用Bogovskiĭ算子推广了Fefferman, Hajduk和Robinson在光滑有界域上的同时逼近理论；对一般无界域则利用Stokes算子的预解算子（Farwig-Kozono-Sohr发展）获得同时逼近结果，并推广了经典的Lions-Magenes引理。作为应用，在R^d（d=2,3）及一般无界域上建立了Leroy-Hopf弱解与不可压缩对流Brinkman-Forchheimer（CBF）方程的能量等式，覆盖临界和超临界情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有无散度同时逼近理论主要在光滑有界域上建立，而不可压缩流体方程（如CBF方程）的能量等式问题在全空间及一般无界域上需要新的数学工具。该工作旨在将有界域上的逼近框架扩展至全空间和一般无界域，以解决这些域上的能量等式问题。

Method: (1) 全空间情形：利用Bogovskiĭ算子构造无散度向量值逼近函数；(2) 一般无界域情形：利用与Stokes算子关联的预解算子（Farwig-Kozono-Sohr方法）构造逼近；(3) 证明推广的Lions-Magenes引理；(4) 将这些同时逼近框架应用于CBF方程的弱解分析，在R^d（d=2,3）上建立能量等式。

Result: 成功地在全空间R^d（d>=2）和一般C^{1,1}型无界域上建立了无散度同时逼近定理；建立了推广的Lions-Magenes引理；作为应用，在R^d（d=2,3）上（覆盖临界与超临界区域）以及一般无界域上，证明了CBF方程Leroy-Hopf弱解的能量等式成立。

Conclusion: 本文将从平滑有界域上建立的同时逼近框架成功推广到全空间和一般无界域，克服了无界域缺少紧性及边界处理的困难，为无界域上流体方程能量等式问题的研究提供了新方法，并推广了Lions-Magenes引理这一有独立价值的分析工具。

Abstract: We construct divergence-free, vector-valued approximation functions on the whole space $\mathbb{R}^d$, $d\geq 2$, as well as on general unbounded domains of uniform $\mathrm{C}^{1,1}$-type. These approximations converge simultaneously in both Sobolev and Lebesgue spaces. In the whole-space setting, we employ Bogovskiĭ\ operators to construct such approximations, thereby extending the approximation theory developed for smooth bounded domains and the simultaneous approximation framework introduced by \emph{Fefferman, Hajduk, and Robinson, {Proc. Lond. Math. Soc.} (3) \ {125} (2022), no.~4, 759-777}. As an application, we establish energy equality for Leray-Hopf weak solutions of the incompressible convective Brinkman-Forchheimer (CBF) equations on $\mathbb{R}^d$, $d\in\{2,3\}$ covering both the critical and supercritical regimes. For general unbounded domains, we employ the resolvent operator associated with the Stokes operator, developed by \emph{Farwig, Kozono and Sohr, {Acta Math.}, {195} (2005), 21-53}, to obtain simultaneous approximation results. We also establish a generalized version of the classical Lions-Magenes lemma, which is of independent interest. Finally, by combining this result with the simultaneous approximation framework for unbounded domains, we establish energy equality for weak solutions of the CBF equations on general unbounded domains.

</details>


### [109] [Sharp convergence rates for the vanishing discount problem with hyperbolic Aubry sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02779)
*Panrui Ni*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了Tonelli哈密顿系统的退化贴现问题（vanishing discount problem），证明了当提升的Aubry集为有限个双曲平衡点或双曲周期轨道之并时，解u_λ向所选临界解u_0的收敛速率为O(λ)与O(λ|log λ|)之间的界，并在特定条件下达到O(λ)；同时通过有限阶和无限阶退化例子展示了这些速率的优化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Tonelli哈密顿系统的退化贴现问题中，需要定量刻画黏性解u_λ当贴现因子λ→0时向临界解的收敛速率，此前缺乏系统的定量理论，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 利用提升Aubry集的几何结构（有限个双曲平衡点或双曲周期轨道的并），结合Mather测度投影和双曲动力系统的性质，建立上界估计；通过构造有限阶与无限阶退化实例建立相应的下界，证明速率的精确性。

Result: 证明了收敛速率满足-Cλ≤u_λ-u_0≤Cλ|log λ|；当每个Γ_i对应的投影Mather测度满足∫u_0 dμ_i=0时，收敛速率提升为O(λ)。单双曲平衡点或单双曲周期轨道情形速率恰为O(λ)，并给出反例说明O(λ)与O(λ|log λ|)均为最优；非双曲情形给出λ^{1/(2r-1)}的下界（r≥2），无限阶退化情形可达任意慢的收敛。

Conclusion: 该工作首次为Tonelli框架下的退化贴现问题提供了系统性的定量收敛速率理论，完整刻画了双曲情形下的最优收敛阶，并揭示了非双曲退化对收敛速率的影响，建立了从O(λ)到任意慢收敛的完整谱系。

Abstract: Let $H\in C^2(T^*M)$ be a Tonelli Hamiltonian on a closed connected manifold and let $u_λ$ solve \[
  λu_λ+H(x,Du_λ)=c(H)\qquad\text{in }M. \] We study the convergence rate of $u_λ$ to the selected critical solution $u_0$. Assume that the lifted Aubry set is a finite union $\widetilde{A}=Γ_1\sqcup\cdots\sqcupΓ_N$, where each $Γ_i$ is either a hyperbolic equilibrium or a periodic orbit hyperbolic in the critical energy level. We prove \[
  -Cλ\le u_λ-u_0\le Cλ|\logλ|. \] Let $μ_i$ be the projected Mather measure associated with $Γ_i$. If \[
  \int_M u_0\,\mathrm dμ_i=0\qquad \text{for every }i, \]
  then \[
  \|u_λ-u_0\|_\infty\le Cλ. \] In particular, if the lifted Aubry set consists of a single hyperbolic equilibrium or a single hyperbolic periodic orbit, the convergence rate is $O(λ)$. We give examples showing that both convergence rates $O(λ)$ and $O(λ|\logλ|)$ are optimal. Without hyperbolicity, finite-order degenerate examples give lower bounds of order $λ^{1/(2r-1)}$ with $r\ge2$. We also construct infinite-order degenerate examples with arbitrarily slow convergence. Taken together, these results provide, to our knowledge, the first systematic quantitative theory for the vanishing discount problem in the Tonelli setting.

</details>


### [110] [Maximizing the fundamental Laplace--Neumann eigenvalue on quadrilaterals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02784)
*Ryoki Endo,Braxton Osting*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文证明在等面积的凸平面四边形中，正方形唯一最大化第一个非零Laplace–Neumann特征值。这是Pólya–Szegő猜想（等面积n边形中正n边形最小化Dirichlet特征值）的Neumann类比在四边形情形下的证明。证明采用Rayleigh–Ritz下界、对称约化Hessian的闭式计算，以及基于证书的盒覆盖方法，分别实现局部和整体的最大化验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Pólya–Szegő猜想是几何谱分析中长期悬而未决的经典问题，断言等面积n边形中正n边形最小化第一个Laplace–Dirichlet特征值。本文关注其Neumann类比，即在等面积四边形中正方形最大化第一个非零Laplace–Neumann特征值。这一结果填补了该类比在四边形情形下的空白，并展示了通过严格计算验证建立几何谱不等式的新方法。

Method: 证明方法分为几个关键步骤：（1）利用正方形前五个非恒定Neumann模态的张成空间，构造一个光滑辅助泛函的Rayleigh–Ritz下界，该泛函的最小化蕴含原始最大化问题；（2）通过对称约化的Hessian矩阵的闭式表达式证明正方形的局部极大性，并结合经过认证的二阶差分商检验在正方形周围显式球内建立局部不等式；（3）通过参数空间的认证盒覆盖建立全局极大性，对每个盒直接认证第一个Rayleigh–Ritz特征值的上界。所有认证均基于固定参考正方形上的积分，避免了对扰动四边形使用后验有限元估计。

Result: 成功证明了在等面积的凸平面四边形中，正方形唯一地最大化第一个非零Laplace–Neumann特征值，即Neumann类比的Polyá–Szegő猜想在四边形情形下成立。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了Neumann类比的Pólya–Szegő猜想在四边形情形的正确性，所发展的认证计算方法（包括对称约化Hessian、二阶差分商检验及盒覆盖认证）为推广到一般n边形提供了可行的技术框架。

Abstract: We prove that among convex planar quadrilaterals of equal area, the square uniquely maximizes the first nonzero Laplace--Neumann eigenvalue. This is the quadrilateral case of the Neumann analogue of the long-standing Pólya--Szegő conjecture, which asserts that the regular $n$-gon minimizes the first Laplace--Dirichlet eigenvalue among $n$-gons of equal area. The proof uses a Rayleigh--Ritz lower bound, obtained from the span of the first five nonconstant Neumann modes of the square, on a smooth auxiliary functional whose minimization implies the original maximization. Local maximality at the square follows from a symmetry-reduced Hessian, computed in closed form, together with a certified second-order difference-quotient test that establishes the local inequality on an explicit ball around the square. Global maximality is then established by a certified box covering of the parameter space, certifying on each box a direct upper bound on the first Rayleigh--Ritz eigenvalue. Both certifications operate entirely on integrals over a fixed reference square and avoid a posteriori finite-element bounds on the perturbed quadrilateral.

</details>


### [111] [Global smooth behavior in Kuznetsov and Westervelt type viscous wave equations: A unifying approach covering $W^{1,q}$-small initial data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02795)
*Tahir Boudjeriou,Michael Winkler*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了一般粘性波动方程在光滑有界域中的初边值问题，证明了在仅涉及一阶Sobolev空间的小性条件下，全局经典解的存在性以及解的指数衰减性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献已在二阶及高阶Sobolev空间中建立了足够小初始数据下全局解的存在性结果，但在仅涉及一阶Sobolev空间的小性条件下全局可解性是否成立这一问题仍然开放。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 针对一般粘性波动方程h(u,u_t)u_tt = ∇u_t + a∇u + f(u,u_t,∇u,∇u_t)，在满足h(0,0)>0、f(0,0,0,0)=0且∇f(0,0,0,0)=0的光滑非线性条件下，通过在一阶Sobolev空间框架下的分析论证，建立了全局经典解的存在性。

Result: 证明了全局经典解的存在性，并获得了(u,u_t)在W^{1,r}×W^{1,p} Sobolev空间中关于时间的指数衰减率。

Conclusion: 该结果将此前仅在二阶或更高阶Sobolev空间中成立的小初始数据全局可解性理论，推广到了一阶Sobolev空间的小性条件框架下，拓展了Kuznetsov和Westervelt型非线性声学方程解的理论基础。

Abstract: In a smoothly bounded domain $\Om\subset\R^n$ with $n\geq 1$ and $a>0$, we consider an initial-boundary value problem for the general viscous wave equation \bas
  h(u,u_t) u_{tt} = \Del u_t + a\Del u + f(u,u_t,\na u,\na u_t) \eas which appears in models of nonlinear acoustics wave propagation; well-established equations of Kuznetsov and Westervelt type form particular examples.\abs %
While the existing literature offers extensive results on global solutions for sufficiently small initial data $(u_{0}, u_{0t})=(u, u_{t})|_{t=0}$ in second- and higher-order Sobolev spaces it appears to remain open how far global solvability can be established under smallness conditions involving only first-order Sobolev spaces. The present manuscript addresses this question by proving the existence of global classical solutions together with exponential decay of the pair $(u,u_{t})$ in $ W^{1,r}\times W^{1,p}$-Sobolev spaces whenever the nonlinearities $h$ and $f$ are sufficiently smooth and are such that $h(0,0)>0$ as well as $f(0,0,0,0)=0$ and $\na f(0,0,0,0)=0$.

</details>


### [112] [Global Well-posedness and Asymptotic Analysis of a Damped Nonlinear Wave Equation with a Codimension-One Constraint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02842)
*Harsh Tiwari,Manil T. Mohan*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个约束版本的阻尼非线性波动方程在L²单位球面（Hilbert流形M）上的强解全局存在唯一性、Hilbert流形不变性、stationary解的计数、长时行为以及收敛到基态解的渐近性态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非约束的阻尼非线性波动方程已在不同背景下被广泛研究，但约束版本（即演化被投影到L²单位球面的切空间上）在数学上尚未得到充分探索。研究此问题不仅丰富约束PDE的理论，且其结果（如stationary解的多重性和长时收敛性）对理解非线性约束动力学具有重要意义。

Method: 采用Faedo–Galerkin逼近方法结合适当的先验估计与紧致性论证，建立全局强解的适定性；利用Lusternik–Schnirelmann理论证明stationary问题具有可数无穷多个stationary解；运用Webb定理与Barbalat引理证明解沿子序列收敛到stationary解；在初值接近第一特征函数的条件下证明H₀¹收敛到唯一的正基态解。

Result: 在给定的p范围条件下，建立了约束阻尼非线性波动方程在Hilbert流形M上的全局强解存在唯一性；证明了M在流作用下不变，L²约束在整个演化过程中保持；stationary问题至少存在可数多个stationary解；解的（子序列）轨线收敛到stationary解；当初值足够接近第一特征函数时，解在H₀¹(Ω)中收敛到唯一的正基态解。

Conclusion: 本文为约束阻尼非线性波动方程建立了完整的全局适定性理论，揭示了L²单位球面流形在该动力学系统中的不变性，并在stationary解多重性与解的长时间收敛性方面给出了深刻的结果，为后续进一步研究此类约束PDE系统奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: We prove the global existence and uniqueness of strong solutions to a constrained version of the damped nonlinear wave equation $$ \vartheta_{tt}+γ\vartheta_t-Δ\vartheta+|\vartheta|^{p-2}\vartheta=0 $$
  on a smooth bounded domain $\varOmega\subset\mathbb{R}^d$, where the evolution is projected onto the tangent space of the Hilbert manifold $$\mathcal{M} = \left\{ \vartheta\in L^2(\varOmega):\|\vartheta\|_{L^2(\varOmega)}=1 \right\}, $$
  which is the unit sphere in $L^2(\varOmega)$. We assume that
  $$p\in[2,\infty)\ \text{ for }\ d=1,2, \ \text{ while }\ 2\leq p\leq \frac{2(d-1)}{d-2} \ \text{ for }\ d\geq3.$$ By employing the Faedo--Galerkin approximation method, together with suitable a priori estimates and compactness arguments, we establish the global well-posedness of the problem. In particular, we show that the Hilbert manifold $\mathcal{M}$ is invariant under the flow, and hence the $L^2$-constraint is preserved throughout the evolution. Using the \emph{Lusternik--Schnirelmann theory}, we show that the corresponding stationary problem possesses at least countably many stationary solutions. We further investigate the long-time behaviour of solutions and prove that, along a subsequence, every solution of the constrained problem converges to a stationary solution by invoking \emph{Webb's theorem} and \emph{Barbalat's lemma}. When the initial data are sufficiently close to the first eigenfunction of the associated stationary problem, we show that the unique strong solution converges in $H_0^1(\varOmega)$ to the unique positive ground-state solution.

</details>


### [113] [On the cut locus of Hamilton--Jacobi equations I: structure and propagation via the touching approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02879)
*Piermarco Cannarsa,Wei Cheng,Jiahui Hong \and Wenxue Wei*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究半凹函数的割迹（cut locus），通过接触方法引入割迹并证明其与Lax-Oleinik半群定义的变分割迹一致。作者定义了正则度R_φ来度量C^{1,1}偏差，证明尖点与近端子微分消失的等价性，并建立了R_φ(x)与割时函数τ_{φ,H}(x)之间的定量关系，由此推导出共轭点不会出现在割迹之前的分离估计。割点沿广义特征全局传播，Alexandrov点沿校准曲线向前传播且二阶导数满足矩阵Riccati方程。研究还证明割迹是Lebesgue零测集，并给出弱KAM解的割迹闭集的充要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 割迹理论在Hamilton-Jacobi方程、黎曼几何和最优控制理论中具有核心地位。此前割迹的两个主要定义途径——接触方法和变分方法（基于Lax-Oleinik半群）——在一般情况下是否等价尚不明确。此外，割迹与共轭点之间的关系、割点的传播性质以及割迹的测度性质等定量问题缺乏系统性研究。作者旨在建立一个统一的割迹理论框架，给出严格的解析判据和定量刻画。

Method: 本文采用接触（touching）方法定义割迹，其解析判据为近端子微分（proximal subdifferential）的空集性。作者引入正则度R_φ(x)度量C^{1,1}偏离程度，证明R_φ(x)与割时函数τ_{φ,H}(x)成反比关系（点态成立，带有显式截断常数）。据此推导校准曲线的分离估计，表明割迹之前不存在共轭点。传播结果通过广义特征和校准曲线获得，二阶导数满足矩阵Riccati方程。割迹的Lebesgue零测性质通过流线论证和Alexandrov定理的精简证明获得。割迹闭集的充要条件用割时函数、C^{1,1}支撑和正则度刻画。

Result: (1) 接触割迹与变分割迹（Lax-Oleinik半群定义）对所有Hamilton量均一致；(2) 尖点等价于近端子微分空集；(3) 建立R_φ(x)∼1/τ_{φ,H}(x)的定量关系；(4) 证明割迹前无共轭点的分离估计；(5) 割点沿广义特征全局传播，Alexandrov点沿校准曲线向前传播且满足Riccati方程；(6) 割迹是Lebesgue零测集；(7) 给出弱KAM解割迹闭集的充要条件。

Conclusion: 本文建立了半凹函数割迹理论的统一框架，弥合了接触视角与变分视角的定义差异，并通过正则度R_φ与割时函数τ_{φ,H}的反比关系将割迹理论定量化。这些工具不仅揭示了割迹与共轭点的几何关系、尖点在动力系统中的传播规律，还推进了对弱KAM解结构（特别是其割迹拓扑性质）的理解，为Hamilton-Jacobi方程的奇性传播分析提供了系统的理论武器。

Abstract: For a semiconcave function with linear modulus, we introduce the cut locus through a touching approach and prove that it coincides with the variational cut locus defined via the Lax--Oleinik semigroup, independently of the Hamiltonian. Cut points are characterized by the emptiness of the proximal subdifferential of $φ$, which we use as the analytic criterion behind the touching definition. We introduce the degree of regularity $R_φ$, which measures the $C^{1,1}$ deviation of $φ$, and prove the quantitative relation $R_φ(x)\sim 1/τ_{φ,H}(x)$ with the cut time function, valid pointwise up to explicit truncation constants. From this relation we derive a separation estimate for calibrated curves, showing that no conjugate points occur before the cut locus. This estimate supplies the tools for the propagation results. Cut points propagate globally along generalized characteristics for the evolutionary Hamilton--Jacobi equation, and Alexandrov points propagate forward along calibrated curves, with the second derivative satisfying a matrix Riccati equation. We also show that the cut locus is a Lebesgue null set and give a streamlined proof of Alexandrov's theorem. Finally, we give necessary and sufficient conditions for the cut locus of a weak KAM solution to be closed, in terms of the cut time function, the $C^{1,1}$ support, and the degree of regularity.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [114] [WMLLM: Self-Evolving Optimization Agents via Predict-Then-Act World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01608)
*Zhongzheng Li,Qingsong Ran,Shikun Feng,Nian Ran,Wenhao Li,Xiaoyuan Zhang,Yue Wang,Xiaoguang Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出WMLLM，一个基于"先预测后行动"世界建模的自进化优化智能体框架，通过大语言模型的隐式知识与强化学习提升黑箱优化任务的样本效率和最终性能，在多目标分子优化基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑箱优化问题因搜索空间大、结构弱、维度高而具有挑战性。现有方法依赖直接候选生成或试错式改进，样本效率低下。作者观察到LLM能凭借隐式知识以较高准确率预测候选解的结果，因此希望通过世界建模在代价高昂的评估前识别有前景的优化方向，从而提升搜索效率。

Method: 提出WMLLM框架，遵循"预测-行动"（predict-then-act）范式：智能体先用世界模型预测有前景的方向，再据此生成候选解。框架整合智能体多轮交互细化、基于种群的搜索以及强化学习，在搜索过程中同时细化隐式世界模型和优化策略，实现自进化能力。

Result: 在黑箱优化任务上，尤其是在多目标分子优化中，WMLLM在有限评估预算下显著提升了样本效率和最终优化性能，在多目标分子优化基准上取得了最先进的（SOTA）结果。

Conclusion: 利用LLM隐式知识构建世界模型进行先预测后行动的策略，能有效引导黑箱搜索避开低效试错，兼顾样本效率与优化性能，验证了世界建模+自进化智能体框架在大规模复杂优化问题上的潜力。

Abstract: Black-box optimization problems remain challenging because of large, weakly structured, and high-dimensional search spaces. Existing methods often suffer from poor sample efficiency because they rely on direct candidate generation or trial-and-error refinement. A natural way to improve search efficiency is to use world modeling, which can help identify promising optimization directions before costly evaluation. Large language models can predict the outcomes of these candidates with nontrivial accuracy because of their implicit knowledge. Motivated by this observation, we propose WMLLM, a self-evolving optimization-agent framework based on predict-then-act world modeling. The agent first predicts promising directions and then acts to generate candidates. Combined with agentic multi-turn refinement, population-based search, and reinforcement learning, WMLLM refines both its implicit world model and its optimization strategy during search. Experiments on black-box optimization tasks, especially multi-objective molecular optimization, show that WMLLM improves sample efficiency and final optimization performance. On the multi-objective molecular optimization benchmark, WMLLM achieves state-of-the-art results under a limited evaluation budget.

</details>


### [115] [DiDrive: A Risk-Aware Hierarchical Diffusion Framework for Safe Offline Reinforcement Learning in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01609)
*Qisong Guo,Jingtang Chen,Zhilin Chen,Pei Xu,Mingjian Fu,Wenxi Liu,Yuanlong Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DiDrive，一个分布引导的离线扩散框架，结合风险感知分层扩散架构(RHDif)和3DICE策略优化范式，在CARLA基准上超越IQL、CQL和Diffusion-QL等基线，尤其在高密度交通场景（60辆车）中实现85%成功率和4295.68平均奖励。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型虽能有效捕捉自动驾驶的多模态行为先验，但离线强化学习策略仍存在分布偏移、重尾风险信号、OOD动作生成以及高维状态冗余等问题，需要针对性解决以提高鲁棒性。

Method: 提出双组件协同框架DiDrive：(1)风险感知分层扩散架构(RHDif)在状态空间利用低级风险门控编码器和高级上下文调制器过滤环境冗余并聚焦安全关键威胁；(2)3DICE策略优化范式在动作空间通过样本内校准引导、时空优化和集成候选排序来缓解OOD过估计与梯度振荡。

Result: 在CARLA基准上优于IQL、CQL和Diffusion-QL等基线，在60辆车辆的高密度复杂交通场景下实现85%的成功率和4295.68的平均奖励。

Conclusion: DiDrive为安全自动驾驶决策提供了稳健的解决方案，表明结合分布引导的风险感知架构与改进的策略优化范式能有效应对离线强化学习在复杂动态场景中的多重挑战。

Abstract: While diffusion models effectively capture multimodal behavioral priors for autonomous driving, offline reinforcement learning (RL) policies remain susceptible to distribution shift, heavy-tailed risk signals, out-of-distribution (OOD) action generation, and high-dimensional state redundancy. To address these challenges, we propose DiDrive, a distribution-guided offline diffusion framework featuring two synergistic components: the Risk-Aware Hierarchical Diffusion (RHDif) architecture and the 3DICE policy optimization paradigm. In the state space, RHDif utilizes a low-level risk-gated encoder and a high-level contextual modulator to filter environmental redundancy and focus on safety-critical threats. In the action space, 3DICE mitigates OOD overestimation and gradient oscillation through in-sample calibrated guidance, spatiotemporal optimization, and ensemble-based candidate ranking. Evaluations on the CARLA benchmark demonstrate DiDrive's superiority over baselines like IQL, CQL, and Diffusion-QL, particularly in complex, high-density traffic scenarios with 60 vehicles, where it achieves an 85% success rate and a 4295.68 average reward, providing a robust pathway for safe autonomous driving decision-making.

</details>


### [116] [Prompt-Space Meta-Learning Does Not Transfer Across Users: A Frozen-LLM Negative Result](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01615)
*Liam Byrne,David Dylan,Orla Fitzgerald,Eoin Doyle,Ciara Nolan,Padraig Lynch,Sinead Gallagher*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文质疑LLM个性化元学习中"元目标可编码可迁移的跨用户适应"这一假设，并提出Muse方法（通过思考型提示进化在元训练用户群上进化单一共享适应提示，冻结后零样本应用于留出用户），同时设计匹配对照来隔离学习与措辞/选择的混淆。在LaMP-2和LaMP-3基准上各200个留出用户的实验表明，Muse未显著优于其未进化的种子提示或结构破坏对照（在错配用户支持对上元训练），并在评分任务上被朴素少样本检索显著超越。作者将结果归因于"元目标坍缩"机制：元验证目标在统计上对用户-支持对应是否真实不变，因此无法被优化为可迁移适应，只能奖励指令打磨和验证过拟合。种子提示、错误支持和不变性预言机对照构成一个可复用协议，用于区分学习的适应与这些混淆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化冻结大模型常被框架化为提示空间中的元学习问题，但该领域很少检验优化的元目标编码的是可迁移的跨用户适应还是通用指令质量。本文旨在通过严格对照实验回答这一根本性问题。

Method: 提出Muse方法，通过反思式提示进化在元训练用户群上进化单一共享适应提示，冻结后零样本应用于留出用户。采用三类匹配对照：未进化种子提示对照、结构破坏对照（在错配用户-支持对上元训练）以及不变性预言机对照，以隔离真实学习适应与措辞/选择混淆。在LaMP-2（分类）和LaMP-3（评分）各200个留出用户上进行评估。

Result: Muse在两类任务上均未显著优于其未进化种子提示或错配支持对照；在评分任务上被朴素少样本检索显著支配（Delta MAE +0.175, p<0.001）。元验证目标对用户-支持对应是否真实统计不变（LaMP-2 p=0.555, LaMP-3 p=0.622），验证了"元目标坍缩"假说。

Conclusion: 共享提示词空间的元个性化面临"元目标坍缩"问题：元验证目标无法区分真实与错配的用户-支持对应关系，只能优化指令打磨和验证过拟合，而非可迁移的跨用户适应。所提出的三对照协议可作为可复用的评估框架，用于区分元个性化中的学习适应与混淆因素。

Abstract: Personalizing a frozen large language model (LLM) to individual users is often framed as a meta-learning problem in prompt space: each user is a task, and one seeks a shared natural-language adaptation policy that, given a handful of the user's labeled interactions, configures the frozen model for that user. The framing is attractive because it is backbone-agnostic and reuses the machinery of prompt optimization, yet the field rarely tests whether the optimized meta-objective encodes transferable cross-user adaptation rather than generic instruction quality. We study this question with Muse (Meta-learned User-adaptation via Shared Evolution), which evolves a single shared adaptation prompt over a meta-train user population by reflective prompt evolution, freezes it, and applies it zero-shot to held-out users; matched controls isolate learning from confounds of phrasing and selection.
